- **Anatomy.** A frame: `┄ [ language ] ┄┄┄ meta`, code rows on `surface` from column 2, a closing `┄` rule. Columns 0 and 1 of code rows are blank and on the base background. No line numbers, no side borders.
- **Syntax colour.** Almost none: code is text, comments are muted. In a diff, added rows are ok-green with `+` in column 2, removed rows are err-pink with `-`, context rows have a space. The sign is part of the row, so a copied diff is a valid patch body.
- **States.** *Short*: identical, never inlined. *Long*: every row is drawn; an answer is never cut. From 80 rows up, the closing rule repeats the label. *Several languages*: one frame each, 1 blank row between.
- **Long lines.** A row longer than the measure continues on the next row with `↩` in column 0.
- **Streaming.** The label rule draws when the fence opens; the file name and line count go on the closing rule when streamed. Rows append.
- **Copy.** Selecting rows yields the code with a uniform 2-space indent. `/copy code n` puts fence *n* of the last answer on the clipboard without the indent (E.24).
- **Site.** `AssistantMessage`.

### E.9 Links, footnotes and Sources · changed

**Purpose.** Links without OSC 8: readable in the sentence, complete and selectable at the end. The answer's sources and the pane's sources are one numbered list seen from two sides.

```text
  One step keeps GitHub's name: the image pull is inserted by GitHub itself¹,   
  and reusable workflows inherit the caller's run-name².                        
                                                                                
┄ [ SOURCES ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 2 
  ¹ docs.github.com  Workflow syntax for GitHub Actions                         
    https://docs.github.com/en/actions/writing-workflows/workflow-syntax        
  ² docs.github.com  Reusing workflows                                          
    https://docs.github.com/en/actions/sharing-automations/reusing-workflows    
┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 
```

- **In prose.** A Markdown link draws its text followed by a superscript mark in link-blue. A bare URL in prose is drawn as `host/…/last-segment` plus a mark. Marks are numbered in order of first appearance, which is stable while streaming.
- **The frame, on the left.** One entry per mark, and only for marks: mark, host (web-teal), page title; then the full URL on its own row, underlined, link-blue, from column 4. A URL row is never cut: Windows Terminal makes a plain URL clickable only when it is unbroken. A URL longer than the row wraps at the last `/`, `?` or `&` that fits, with `↩` in the spine.
- **What changed.** v6 listed pages that were read but not cited in this frame. They now live only in the pane (E.43). The frame is answer content: it holds exactly what the answer stands on, it is selectable, and it survives without a pane. The pane's `[ SOURCES ]` is the reading log: every page fetched, with its HTTP status, as it happens.
- **The relationship.** While the turn runs, the pane lists each page with `·`. When the answer cites a page, the same row gains the answer's mark. So `¹` in a sentence, `¹` in the frame and `¹` in the pane are the same page, and a `·` row in the pane is a page Claude read and did not use.
- **States.** *Footnotes only* (links, no web work): the frame is labelled `[ LINKS ]` and the pane has no `[ SOURCES ]` section.
- **Streaming.** Marks appear with their sentence. The frame draws once, when the block settles.
- **Narrow.** Host and title share a row until 60 columns; below that the title moves to its own row.
- **Site.** `AssistantMessage` (the final block of the turn); the pane rows come from `tool.call` on the web tools.

### E.10 Figures · kept

**Purpose.** Framed ASCII figures (mdxcn fences) arrive with their own frame. Cockpit does not add a second one and never reflows them.

```text
  +------------------ [ NIGHT ] -------------------+                            
  |                                                |                            
  | ●  14:02  p95 crossed 800ms                    |                            
  | │                                              |                            
  | ●  14:11  rolled back the cache flag           |                            
  | │                                              |                            
  | ○  14:40  write the postmortem                 |                            
  |                                                |                            
  +------------------------------------------------+                            
  Figure 1 · the night of the incident                                          
```

- **Detection.** A fenced block with no language (or `text`) whose first row matches `+-… [ TITLE ] …-+`.
- **Paint.** Frame characters dim, title muted, body text. Inside a figure the figure's own glyphs keep the figure's meaning; table D governs Cockpit's rows, not quoted art.
- **Background.** None: a figure is not code. **Caption.** A muted row under it when the next paragraph starts with `Figure`.
- **Streaming.** Buffered like a table: a figure draws only when its closing row has arrived.
- **Narrow.** A figure wider than the measure is not wrapped or scaled. It is cut at the frame edge, with one muted row under it: `figure is 64 cells wide · window shows 57`. Docked at 110 columns the transcript is only 67 wide, so this case is more common than it was; the row also says `/cockpit off widens the transcript`.
- **Site.** `AssistantMessage`.

### E.11 Tables · kept

**Purpose.** Data on the grid: no vertical lines, one rule, alignment by type.

```text
  Workflow    Before                         After                              
  ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── 
  scorecard   Pull ghcr.io/ossf/scorecard…   Scorecard · analyze                
  ci          build                          CI · test · ubuntu                 
  ci          lint                           CI · lint · oxlint                 
  pr          check                          PR · lint title                    
```

- **Anatomy.** Header row in muted; a rule-strong `─` rule across the block measure; body rows in text; every second body row on `zebra`. Columns are separated by at least 3 blank cells. No outer frame, no column lines.
- **Column widths** are computed once, from the whole table, before it draws. Each column gets its longest cell up to a cap of 40; remaining width goes to the widest text column; a column that still does not fit wraps its cells to at most 3 rows and then cuts with `…`.
- **Shapes.**

| Shape | Rule |
|---|---|
| plain | as above; text left-aligned |
| numeric | numbers right-aligned on the decimal point; unit in the header; negative uses `−` (U+2212); a total row is preceded by a hairline rule and is bold |
| comparison | first column is the row label in muted; cells are `yes` / `no` / a value, never ticks alone |
| status | status is a word, coloured by meaning: `pass` ok, `fail` err bold, `skip` muted, `flaky` warn |
| key and value | 2 columns, no header and no rule; keys muted, values text |
| before → after | "before" cells muted, "after" cells text; a `→` column between exactly 2 value columns |
| wide | pivots: each row becomes a key and value record under a dotted rule `┄ [ 3 of 12 ] ┄` |
| long (over 40 rows) | every row is drawn; the header and rule repeat every 40 rows |
| empty | header, rule, then one muted row: `no rows` |

- **Streaming.** A table is buffered. One muted row stands in for it at the bottom of the block: `table · 4 rows so far`. When the first non-table line arrives, the placeholder is replaced by the finished table (scenario F.30).
- **Narrow.** The same algorithm with a smaller measure: caps drop to 24, then cells wrap, then the table pivots.
- **Site.** `AssistantMessage`.

### E.12 Tool kinds · changed

One vocabulary for every tool, used in four places: `[ NOW ]`, a `[ STEPS ]` row, an unfolded step row, and (for exceptions and events) a transcript row.

| Kind | Tools | Subject | Result | Mark it can earn in the pane | Transcript row |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `read` | Read, image and PDF reads | path, `:lines` if ranged | `884 lines` | `✕` not found | only if never fixed |
| `edit` | Edit, MultiEdit | path | `+3 −1` | `✕` no match, `⊘` blocked | only if never fixed |
| `write` | Write | path | `+156` | `⊘` blocked | `●` when the path is a memory file (E.26) |
| `search` | Grep, Glob, LSP | pattern, then `in path` | `41 matches` | none: 0 matches is a result | none |
| `shell` | Bash, PowerShell | the command | exit summary: `clean`, `exit 1`, last line of stdout | `✕` non-zero exit, `±` commit, `↗` push, PR, merge | `↗` at once |
| `test` | a shell call whose command is a test runner | the command | `191 pass`, `2 of 14 fail` | `✕` failures, `✓` recovery or final check | only if never fixed |
| `web` | WebSearch | the query in quotes | `8 results` | `✕` | none |
| `fetch` | WebFetch | host and path | `200 · 41 kB` | `✕` 4xx, 5xx, timeout | none; the page is in `[ SOURCES ]` |
| `mcp` | any `mcp__server__tool` | `server · tool` | first line of the result | `✕` | `●` server disconnected |
| `skill` | Skill | skill name | `loaded` | `✕` | none |
| `hook` | a classic hook that ran on a call | event and hook name | `passed` | `⊘` when it blocks | only if never resolved |
| `notebook` | NotebookEdit | path and cell | `cell 12 · +4 −2` | `✕` | only if never fixed |
| `browser` | Playwright and browser tools | verb and target: `click "Sign in"` | `navigated`, `filled`, `saved · 212 kB` | `✕` with the selector as detail | only if never fixed |
| `agent` | Agent | the agent's name | `running`, `done`, `failed` | `▸`, `✕` | only if failed |
| `ask` | AskUserQuestion | the question's header | your answer | `!` waiting, `▍` answered | the answer row (E.16) |
| `plan` | ExitPlanMode | size of the plan | `approved`, `rejected` | `▍` | frame and verdict (E.17) |
| `todo` | TodoWrite | `n items` | `3 of 7` | none | none |

```text
┄ [ STEPS ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 5 steps · 1 failed
  browser  goto localhost:5173/login             200 · 1280×720      58s
  browser  fill #email "dev@ghostframe.test"             filled      59s
  browser  click "Sign in"                            navigated   1m 00s
  browser  screenshot dashboard.png              saved · 212 kB   1m 01s
✕ browser  click "Export CSV"                   no such element   1m 09s
```

- **Screenshots and images** are never drawn. The row names the file.
- **Site.** `tool.call` feeds the pane's state; `ToolUse` and `ToolGroup` draw nothing in the folded transcript (probe 2) and step rows in the unfolded one.

### E.13 Subagents · changed

**Purpose.** Agents are parallel work that you do not want to read. Docked, they live in the pane's roster (E.40) from launch to the end of the turn and leave no transcript row unless one fails for good. This section is the fallback.

```text
┄ [ AGENTS ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 5 running · 2 done [-]     
◇ Audit register.tsx     read    hooks/register.tsx       14 ·  1m 12s          
◇ Audit helper modules   shell   node redos-probe.mjs     22 ·  1m 12s          
◇ Research toolchain     fetch   bun.sh/docs/install       9 ·  1m 10s          
◇ Research CI and suppl… web     "zizmor auditor persona" 11 ·  1m 09s          
◇ Audit tests            done                             31 ·  3m 58s          
✕ Audit publish-readine… failed  context limit            48 ·  4m 02s          
                                                                                
✕ failed · agent Audit publish-readiness · context limit                13m 42s 
│ worked · 7 agents · 214 steps · 6 done · 1 failed                     14m 50s 
```

- **Fallback roster** (live area, frame `[ AGENTS ]`): one row per agent, as in v6: `◇` and name in agent-lavender (22 cells, cut with `…`), the kind word and subject of its current step, its step count, its elapsed time. A nested agent is inset 2 cells; only 2 levels are shown. All 7 rows are shown when the band has room (it is capped at half the terminal's rows **(types)**); otherwise the oldest finished agents collapse into one row.
- **Launch and finish rows are gone.** v6 drew one row for the launch and one per finished agent. In fallback the capsule now carries them: `│ worked · 7 agents · 214 steps · 6 done · 1 failed`. A failed agent still gets its `✕` row at once.
- **Background agents** keep their roster after the turn ends (`[ AGENTS · BACKGROUND ]`), and the receipt says `waiting · n agents`.
- **Messages from agents** (`›`) and the engine's own "agent finished" rows are engine rows: probe 5 decides whether the plugin may draw them as nothing when docked. If not, they stay; they start with `◇` or `›` in column 0, on the grid.
- **Narrow.** Roster drops the subject, then the step count. Below 60 columns it is one row: `◇ 5 running · 2 done`.
- **Site.** `AbovePrompt` (roster); `ToolUse` for `Agent`; `UserMessage` for notifications (probe 5).

### E.14 To-do list · changed

**Purpose.** The plan of record is live. Docked it is the pane's `[ TODO ]` section (E.41) and the transcript shows nothing. This section is the fallback.

```text
▸ [>] Split register.tsx into ledger and held store                  5 of 7     
                                                                                
─ done · turn 12 ──────────────────────────── 5 of 7 to-dos · 9 files · 12m 05s 
```

- **Fallback, live.** One row in the band: `▸`, the item in progress, `n of m`.
- **Fallback, on request.** `/todo` prints the list as a `[ TODO ]` frame, with the marks of that moment. v6 drew that frame automatically at the first write; in Cockpit nothing is written into the conversation that you did not ask for.
- **At turn end**, in both modes, the receipt carries `7 of 7 to-dos`, or `5 of 7 to-dos` in warn if the turn ended early.
- **Site.** `AbovePrompt`; `CommandOutput`; `Pane`.

### E.15 Permission prompts · changed

**Purpose.** The one moment the interface must stop you. The dialog is the engine's and no render site draws it; Cockpit owns everything around it.

```text
┄ [ PERMISSION ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ shell 
  git push --force-with-lease origin fix/rendering-parsing                      
  Rewrites 3 commits on the remote branch. Rebased on main at 2c4afb9.          
                                                                                
  ▸ 1  allow once                                                               
    2  always allow  Bash(git push --force-with-lease:*) in this repo           
    3  deny, and tell Claude what to do instead                                 
┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 
                                                                                
▍ always allow · Bash(bun test:*) · this repo                            2m 10s 
▍ denied · shell rm -rf dist · "keep the build output"                   6m 31s 
```

- **Waiting, docked.** The engine's dialog takes the place of the prompt box. The pane's `[ NOW ]` section turns to `! needs you · permission` with the step that asked on its second row, and that step's row in `[ STEPS ]` carries `!` and `needs you` (scenario F.36). The status line's mode cell says `needs you`. The left now-row disappears: nothing is running. The terminal bell rings once. There is no separate live row: the dialog itself is the call to action, and it is right there.
- **Waiting, fallback.** As v6: a live row `! needs you · permission · <kind> <subject>` above the dialog, because without a pane nothing else names the step (scenario F.9).
- **The dialog** (top of the mock) is the target look if a site ever reaches it: a `[ PERMISSION ]` frame labelled with the kind, the exact subject in text-bright, one line of consequence, numbered options with `▸` and a chip on the selected one. Today it is engine-drawn and takes the theme's colours only (probe 8).
- **Kinds and their subject line.** `shell`: the command. `edit` / `write`: the path and a diff frame cut to 12 rows. `fetch`: the host. `mcp`: `server · tool` and the arguments. `read` outside the project: the absolute path. Destructive commands add the consequence line in warn.
- **Outcomes.**

| Outcome | Transcript | Pane step |
|---|---|---|
| allow once | nothing | result `allowed once`, no mark |
| always allow | `▍ always allow · Bash(bun test:*) · this repo` | result `always allow`, mark `▍` |
| deny | `▍ denied · shell rm -rf dist` and your reason in quotes | result `denied`, mark `▍` |
| skipped by the auto-mode classifier | only if never resolved: `⊘ still blocked · … · auto-mode classifier` | result `classifier`, mark `⊘` |
| blocked by policy or hook | only if never resolved | result names who, mark `⊘` |

- **Site.** Outcome rows: `ToolUse` (`isErrored` is true for "a refusal at the dialog" **(types)**). The pane knows a dialog is up from `isPromptCovered` on its own props **(types)**.

### E.16 Ask-user question cards · changed

**Purpose.** Claude asks, you choose. The card is the engine's dialog wrapped in a frame; the answer becomes one of your rows.

```text
┄ [ QUESTION ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 1 of 1 · single choice 
  PR #1 (build: toolchain, strict types and ci) is green. How should it land?   
                                                                                
  ▸ 1  Squash-merge each PR when green                                          
    2  Merge commits, keep the topic history                                    
    3  Leave it open; I will merge it myself                                    
    4  Other…                                                                   
┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 
                                                                                
▍ How should it land? → Squash-merge each PR when green                  9m 02s 
▍ Stage list → security settings, merge settings                         9m 20s 
```

- **Card.** Frame label `[ QUESTION ]`, meta `n of m · single choice` or `choose any`. The question in text-bright; options numbered; `▸` and a chip on the current option; multi-select uses `[ ]` / `[x]`. The engine's dialog node is kept whole inside the frame: a tree for this site "holds the engine's node once" and at most 12 rows on the terminal **(types)**.
- **While it waits.** The pane's `[ NOW ]` says `! needs you · question · 1 of 1` with the question's header; the step row is an `ask` step with `!` (scenario F.12).
- **Answered.** One `▍` row on the left: the question's header in muted, `→`, your answer in text. In the pane the `ask` step's mark becomes `▍` and its result is your answer. The two say the same thing in two places on purpose: the left is the record, the pane row is the step's result like any other.
- **Skipped or dismissed.** `▍ no answer · How should it land?`
- **Site.** `AskUserQuestion` (the card); `ToolUse` for the tool (the answered row).

### E.17 Plan mode · kept on the left, new on the right

**Purpose.** A plan is a document you approve. It gets the largest frame on the left and a one-row verdict from you.

```text
┄ [ PLAN ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 4 steps · touches 31 files 
  Split register.tsx into a ledger and a held store                             
                                                                                
  1. Extract claim logic into hooks/ledger.ts behind claim(key, turn).          
  2. Move the LRU into hooks/held.ts; test it at the interface.                 
  3. Re-point 19 tests at the new modules, one commit per module.               
  4. Delete the dead exports the audit confirmed.                               
                                                                                
  Not in this plan: the grapheme width fix (PR #4).                             
┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 
                                                                                
▍ approved the plan · accept edits on                                    2m 05s 
```

- **Proposed.** `[ PLAN ]` frame; meta counts steps and files when the plan states them. The body is ordinary answer Markdown.
- **Approved.** `▍ approved the plan · accept edits on` (or `· default mode`). **Rejected.** `▍ rejected the plan` and your reason in quotes; the next plan is a new frame, `[ PLAN · REVISION 2 ]`. **Edited.** `▍ edited the plan · 2 changes`, followed by a `[ PLAN · YOUR EDIT ]` frame.
- **In the pane.** The reads and searches of the exploration are ordinary steps. Each proposal is a `plan` step whose result is your verdict, with `▍`. After approval the idle pane's second row says what is next (scenario F.38).
- **While planning.** The status line's mode cell says `plan`.
- **Site.** `ToolUse` for `ExitPlanMode`. The approval dialog itself is engine-drawn (probe 8).

### E.18 Mode indicator · kept

**Purpose.** Always know who decides: you, a rule, or a classifier. And, new, who has the keyboard.

```text
  default · shift+tab to change · ? for shortcuts                               
  accept edits · shift+tab to change · esc to stop                              
  plan · reads only · shift+tab to change                                       
  auto · a classifier decides · shift+tab to change · esc to stop               
  pane · arrows scroll · tab moves · enter opens · esc returns to the prompt    
```

- **Where.** The hint line under the prompt, starting at column 2: the mode word first, then at most two hints. The same word is in status-line row 1.
- **States.** `default` muted; `accept edits` text; `plan` text-bright; `auto` warn and bold; `bypass` drawn like `auto`.
- **New: `pane`.** While the pane holds the keyboard the hint line says so and lists its keys (last row of the mock), because keys you type no longer reach the prompt. Escape returns them **(types)**.
- **Site.** `PromptHint` (rewrite of `hint`) **(types)**; `SessionMode` for the dim labels at the right of the prompt footer.

### E.19 Interrupted turns · kept

```text
▍ interrupted · esc · shell bun run gate was cut after 22s               4m 12s 
                                                                                
─ interrupted · turn 11 ─────────────────────────── 31 steps · 2 files · 4m 12s 
```

- **Row.** `▍` (it was your act), `interrupted`, how (`esc`, or `by a new message`), and the step that was cut. **Receipt.** State word `interrupted` in warn.
- **Partial answer.** If prose was streaming, it stays as drawn and ends with a muted `…` on its own row.
- **In the pane.** The cut step keeps its row with `▍` and the result `cut`. The idle head says `interrupted · step 31 cut` (scenario F.13).
- **Site.** `ToolUse` (`isInterrupted` **(types)**); `TurnDuration` (probe 3).

### E.20 Queued and steering messages · kept

```text
▍ queued  also bump the plugin version to 0.4.0    sends when the turn ends     
                                                                                
▍ remember to follow skills properly, do not go astray           steer · 5m 10s 
```

- **Queued** (typed while a turn runs, not yet delivered): a live-area row on the left in both modes, `▍ queued` and the text. Your words never go to the pane.
- **Steer** (delivered mid-turn): a normal prompt row with no turn rule above it, and `steer` plus the turn clock on the time edge. The turn number does not change.
- **Site.** `AbovePrompt` (queued); `UserMessage` (steer).

### E.21 Errors · kept

**Purpose.** An error row says what failed, whether anything is still being tried, and what you can do, in that order.

```text
✕ failed · API error 500 · 10 retries over 2m 41s · nothing was lost    14m 49s 
! needs you · rate limited · resumes 14:05 · or /model to switch        15m 02s 
! needs you · 5-hour limit reached · resets 16:00 (in 1h 12m)           15m 02s 
! needs you · weekly limit reached · resets Mon 09:00                   15m 02s 
✕ failed · network · no route to api.anthropic.com                      15m 30s 
✕ failed · shell timed out after 2m 00s · bun test --watch              17m 31s 
```

| Error | While it is being retried | When it is final |
|---|---|---|
| API error, overloaded | `[ NOW ]`, second row: `waiting  API overloaded (529) · retry 3 of 10 in 8s`. Fallback: the live now-row | `✕ failed · API error 5xx · n retries over D · nothing was lost`; receipt `failed` |
| rate limit | `[ NOW ]` with the resume time | `! needs you · rate limited · resumes HH:MM` |
| 5-hour cap, weekly cap | — | `! needs you · … limit reached · resets …`; the status-line meter for that window is full |
| network | `[ NOW ]`: `waiting  network · retry n` | `✕ failed · network · …` |
| tool timeout | — | a failed step like any other (E.5) |

- A retry that succeeds leaves no row anywhere. Retries are counted in the receipt only if they cost more than 30 seconds: `retried 41s`.
- **Site.** Retries: `Spinner` (`message` carries the override text **(types)**). The final rows are engine messages: probe 9.

### E.22 Context warnings and compaction · kept

```text
! needs you · context 92% · /compact now, or it compacts at 95%              [-]
                                                                                
● compacted · 182k → 24k tokens · summary kept · 38 turns folded          23:58 
                                                                                
─ [ TURN 31 · AFTER COMPACT ] ─────────────────────────────────────────── 23:59 
```

- **Warning.** From 80% the status-line context meter is in warn. From 90% the live area gains a `! needs you · context n%` row on the left, in both modes, even when idle. This is the answer to "does CONTEXT earn a pane section": it does not (E.37).
- **Manual and automatic compaction.** One `●` row with before → after tokens. The engine's own notice is left as is. The plugin's row adds `step logs kept`, because the pane's history is the plugin's data, not Claude's context (E.45).
- **After any compaction** the next turn rule is labelled `· AFTER COMPACT`.
- **Site.** `AbovePrompt`; `CommandOutput` for `/compact`; `UserMessage` for the turn rule.

### E.23 Resume and recap · kept

```text
● resumed · session of Wed 18 Nov · 30 turns · 61% context                09:02 
                                                                                
※ recap: You're modernizing and publishing the theme as ghostframe, and I'm     
  fixing what review found on PR #4. (turn off recaps in /config)               
                                                                                
─ [ TURN 31 · RESUMED ] ──────────────────────────────────── Thu 19 Nov · 09:02 
```

- **Resume.** One `●` notice (plugin, at `session.start`), which now also says how many turns have a step log, then the first turn rule carries `· RESUMED` and the date.
- **Recap.** Engine-drawn and unreachable. It begins with `※` at column 0 and continues at column 2, so it is on the grid. Docked, it runs to the divider.
- **Site.** `session.start` hook plus `UserMessage`.

### E.24 Slash commands · changed

**Purpose.** A command's output is a small document about the session, so it is an open frame labelled with the command. Cockpit adds its own commands, which are how you reach from the prompt what the pane does with keys.

```text
┄ [ /cost ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 
  session      $54.26    4h 41m · 31 turns                                      
  last turn    $1.23     51m 25s · 312 steps                                    
  input        1.2M      tokens · 94% cached                                    
  output       186k      tokens                                                 
┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 
                                                                                
● /steps 7 · turn 7 pinned in the pane · 20 steps · esc or /steps to rel… 10:41 
● /cockpit off · pane closed · this session uses capsules                 10:42 
✕ failed · /steps 44 · no such turn · this session has 31                 10:42 
```

- **Echo.** The engine echoes the command as a prompt row; Cockpit draws it as a prompt with no turn rule: commands are not turns.
- **Output.** One row is a `●` notice. More than one row is a frame labelled with the command. Errors (`isErrored` **(types)**) are a `✕` row.
- **Cockpit's commands.**

| Command | Does | Output |
|---|---|---|
| `/cockpit` | opens or closes the pane; having asked once, the engine docks it from 110 columns | one notice |
| `/cockpit 56` | asks for a pane of that many columns (24 to 45% of the terminal) | one notice |
| `/cockpit off` | closes the pane for this session; the transcript uses capsules | one notice |
| `/steps 7` | pins turn 7's steps in the pane (E.45). Where no dock exists it opens the inline inspector (E.46) | one notice |
| `/steps` | releases the pin | one notice |
| `/steps 7 all` | prints turn 7's whole log here, as unfolded rows (E.4) | a frame |
| `/diff <path>` | prints the diff of one file changed this session (E.42) | a diff frame, first hunk; `all` for every hunk |
| `/files [n]` | prints the files-changed table of turn n, or of the last turn | a frame (E.31) |
| `/todo` | prints the to-do list as it stands | a frame |
| `/copy` | the engine's: the last answer. Cockpit adds `code n`, `link n`, `steps n`, `turn n`, `diff <path>` | one notice |

- **Dialogs.** `/model`, `/config`, `/theme`, `/mcp`, `/permissions` open engine dialogs; when they close, the plugin adds one `●` row with the result.
- **Site.** `CommandOutput`; `$.command.register` in `session.start`, answered by `command.run` **(types)**. `command.run` carries `presentation.isFullscreen` and `columns`, so a command knows whether its answer can go to a dock **(types)**.

### E.25 Bash mode (`!`) · kept

```text
▍ ! bun test tests/width.test.tsx --bail                                        
┄ [ shell ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ exit 1 · 1.4s 
  tests/width.test.tsx:                                                         
  (fail) width > counts a combining mark as zero cells [0.41ms]                 
   1 pass                                                                       
   1 fail                                                                       
  … 188 more lines · ctrl+o                                                     
┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 
```

- **Input.** A prompt row whose text starts with an accent `!`. No turn rule.
- **Output.** A `[ shell ]` frame; meta is the exit code and duration, `exit 0` muted, anything else err. Output rows on `surface`, ANSI colours reduced to the palette. At most 12 rows: the last 12 on a non-zero exit, the first 12 otherwise; then one muted row with the remaining count.
- **Not in the pane.** A `!` command is yours and is not a turn, so `[ STEPS ]` does not change (scenario F.18).
- **Site.** `UserMessage` (probe 10).

### E.26 Memory saves · kept

```text
● memory saved · decide-dont-ask.md · feedback · +15 lines               3m 40s 
● memory updated · follow-skills-one-to-one.md · +3 −1                   9m 12s 
```

- A write or edit whose path is inside the memory directory changes future sessions, so it earns a `●` notice on the left in both modes, as well as its ordinary step row in the pane.
- **Site.** `ToolUse` for `Write` / `Edit`, matched on the path.

### E.27 Hook output and hook errors · changed

```text
⊘ still blocked · edit hooks/grid.ts · hook PreToolUse lint-staged       2m 14s 
    oxlint: 2 errors · no-floating-promises (hooks/grid.ts:88, :102)            
● hook SessionStart · caveman · mode: caveman                             23:40 
                                                                                
● ghostframe: turn.start hook skipped: threw state.set: denied: ghostframe own… 
```

- **A hook that passes** is a step of kind `hook`, in the pane only when hooks took more than 1 second in total.
- **A hook that blocks** marks its step `⊘` in the pane, with the hook's name as the result. If the turn ends with the block unresolved, a `⊘ still blocked` row with up to 3 rows of the hook's output is drawn above the receipt (scenario F.19). In fallback the `⊘ blocked` row draws at once.
- **A hook that prints at session start** is one `●` notice per hook, first line only.
- **Plugin hook failures** (last row of the mock) are printed by the engine, dim, one line each, and only while the session hot-reloads a plugin folder. They are a development artefact and the fix is the bug.
- **Site.** `ToolUse`; `TurnDuration`; `session.start` hook; engine.

### E.28 Notices · kept

```text
● mcp context7 disconnected · tools unavailable · /mcp to reconnect       23:12 
● ghostframe reloaded · 13 hooks · pane kept                              23:41 
● update 2.1.329 installed · restart to apply                             23:41 
```

- **Anatomy.** `●` muted, one row, muted text, wall clock on the time edge. Never coloured unless it needs you, in which case it is a `!` row instead.
- **Dedupe.** An identical notice within 10 minutes draws nothing.
- **Reload.** The pane survives a plugin reload: the engine keeps it and `$.ui.panes()` finds it again **(types)**; the notice says `pane kept`.
- **Site.** Rows appended from hooks; `InfoNotice` at startup **(types)**.

### E.29 Checkpoints and rewind · kept

```text
↺ rewound to turn 12 · 3 files restored · conversation kept               23:20 
                                                                                
─ [ TURN 13 · AFTER REWIND ] ──────────────────────────────────────────── 23:21 
```

- **Checkpoints** are silent. **Rewind:** one `↺` row: where to, what was restored. The undone turns stay in the scrollback; the next turn rule says `· AFTER REWIND`.
- **In the pane.** The step logs of undone turns are kept and can still be pinned; their label gains `· REWOUND`.
- **Site.** `CommandOutput` for `/rewind`; `UserMessage`.

### E.30 Git events · changed

```text
↗ pushed fix/naming · 2 commits · origin                                 6m 12s 
↗ PR #5 opened · ci: precise workflow, job and step names                6m 14s 
    https://github.com/tashfiqul-islam/ghostframe/pull/5                        
↗ PR #5 merged · squash · main is a1f3c2e                                8m 09s 
↗ pushed (forced) fix/rendering-parsing · 3 commits rewritten · origin   9m 40s 
```

- **Detection.** From the `shell` call's command and output, not from prose.
- **Rows on the left, both modes:** `↗` push (branch, count, remote); `↗` PR opened (number and title, with the URL on a detail row, because you will want to click it); `↗` merged (strategy and the new head).
- **Not rows any more:** `±` commit and `✓` checks. A commit is a pane mark (`±` and the short hash as the result) and normally a sentence of the answer. Checks are steps; a failed check that passes on a re-run is `1 failure → fixed` in the receipt (scenario F.25).
- **Force push** is a permission prompt first (E.15), and its row says `pushed (forced)` in warn.
- **Site.** `ToolUse`.

### E.31 Files-changed summary · changed

**Purpose.** Docked, the pane's `[ FILES ]` section is the summary (E.42), live. The transcript keeps only the count, in the receipt. This section is what you get on request.

```text
┄ [ /files · TURN 12 ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 4 changed · +212 −38 
  hooks/ledger.ts                                       new    +141             
  hooks/held.ts                                         new     +52             
  hooks/register.tsx                                            +11   −38       
  tests/kit/seed.ts                                              +8             
┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 
```

- **`/files`** prints the table of the last turn, or of turn n: path relative to the project root, a muted word for `new`, `del`, `ren`, then `+n` in ok and `−n` in err, right-aligned in two columns. Sorted by path. More than 12 files: the 11 largest, then `… 19 more files · +88 −41`.
- **No automatic frame.** v6 appended a `[ FILES ]` frame to every turn that changed 2 or more files. The receipt's count and one command replace it.
- **Site.** `CommandOutput`.

### E.32 The receipt · changed

**Purpose.** What every turn leaves behind: one row that says how it ended, what it cost and which turn it was. It is the only trace of the work on the left when docked, and it is enough to find the rest.

```text
─ done · turn 7 ─────────────── 20 steps · 1 failure → fixed · 3 files · 2m 44s 
─ done · t… 312 steps · 3 failures → fixed · 31 files · 7 of 7 to-dos · 51m 25s 
─ failed · turn 9 ─────────────────────────────────── 9 steps · 1 failure · 48s 
─ needs you · turn 10 ─────────────────────────────── 6 steps · 1 blocked · 48s 
─ interrupted · turn 11 ─────────────────────────── 31 steps · 2 files · 4m 12s 
─ waiting · 2 agents · turn 13 ────────────────────────────── 12 steps · 1m 05s 
─ done · turn 14 ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── 0.4s 
                                                                                
─ done · turn 7 ────────────────────────────────────────────── 3 files · 2m 44s 
```
```paint
h mmmm m mmmm m hhhhhhhhhhhhhhh mm mmmmm m m mmmmmmm m mmmmm m m mmmmm m tt ttt 
h mmmm m mm mmm mmmmm m m mmmmmmmm m mmmmm m mm mmmmm m m mm m mmmmmm m ttt ttt 
h RRRRRR m mmmm m hhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh m mmmmm m m mmmmmmm m ttt 
h WWWWW WWW m mmmm mm hhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh m mmmmm m m mmmmmmm m ttt 
h wwwwwwwwwww m mmmm mm hhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh mm mmmmm m m mmmmm m tt ttt 
h vvvvvvv v v vvvvvv m mmmm mm hhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh mm mmmmm m tt ttt 
h mmmm m mmmm mm hhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh tttt 
                                                                                
h mmmm m mmmm m hhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhhh m mmmmm m tt ttt 
```
```bg
................................................................................
................................................................................
................................................................................
................................................................................
................................................................................
................................................................................
................................................................................
................................................................................
................................................................................
```

- **Fields**, in order, right-aligned as one group ending on the time edge: steps; exceptions (`1 failure → fixed`, `1 failure`, `1 blocked`, `1 denied`, `1 agent failed`); agents; files; sources; plans; to-dos; `thought 48s` when thinking took more than 10 seconds; duration (text; everything else muted).
- **State word** at column 2, then `· turn n`: `done` (muted), `failed` (err, bold), `interrupted` (warn), `needs you` (warn, bold), `waiting · 2 agents` (agent).
- **Dropped since v6:** the by-kind breakdown (the pane and the capsule have it) and tokens (the status line has context).
- **Fallback** (last row of the mock). The capsule already said the steps, so the receipt does not, unless the turn left more than one capsule, in which case it gives the total.
- **Zero steps:** the steps field is omitted. **Under 1 second:** `0.4s`.
- **Narrow.** Fields drop in this order: thought, to-dos, plans, sources, files, agents, steps. Exceptions, the state word, the turn number and the duration always fit: at 30 columns the receipt is `─ failed · turn 9 ─ 1 failure · 48s`.
- **Is it a button?** If `TurnDuration`'s tree may hold a `Button`, a click on a receipt pins that turn (probe 25). The design does not depend on it.
- **Site.** `TurnDuration`. It provides `durationMs` and `waitingFor` **(types)**; the counts come from the plugin's own step log.

### E.33 The live area · changed

**Purpose.** Everything that is true only now and belongs near the prompt. Docked, almost all of it moved to the pane.

Docked: the left says that the turn is alive and for how long. Nothing else.

```text
⠹ working                                                               12m 08s 
▍ queued  also bump the plugin version to 0.4.0                                 
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> █                                                                             
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  auto · a classifier decides · shift+tab to change · esc to stop               
```

Fallback: the whole of v6's live area, unchanged.

```text
⠹ test     bun test tests/width.test.tsx            step 47 · 8s · 12m 08s      
┄ [ AGENTS ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 2 running [-]     
◇ Audit register.tsx     read    hooks/register.tsx       14 ·  1m 12s          
◇ Research toolchain     fetch   bun.sh/docs/install       9 ·  2m 03s          
▸ [>] Split register.tsx into ledger and held store                  5 of 7     
▍ queued  also bump the plugin version to 0.4.0                                 
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> █                                                                             
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  auto · a classifier decides · shift+tab to change · esc to stop               
```

What moved out, docked:

| v6 live area | Docked, it is now | Why |
|---|---|---|
| now-row: spinner, kind, subject, `step n`, step time, turn time | spinner, `working`, turn time on the left; kind, subject, step number and step time in `[ NOW ]` | "alive, and for how long" belongs where your eyes are when you type; "doing what" is the pane's first job |
| `[ AGENTS ]` roster | pane | it needed up to 9 rows above the prompt |
| current to-do row | pane | |
| `! needs you · permission` | the dialog, `[ NOW ]` and the status line | the dialog is already at the prompt |
| `! needs you · context n%` | stays on the left | it is not about a step |
| `▍ queued` | stays on the left | your words |

- **The word** on the docked now-row is `working`, or `writing` while the answer streams. It is deliberately not the kind word: that would say the pane's fact twice.
- **Height.** Docked the band is 1 row, or 2 with a queued message. Undocked it follows v6: at most `min(maxRows, 9)`, collapsing finished agents first.
- **Idle.** No now-row in either mode.
- **Site.** Now-row: `Spinner` (a hook may draw "a tree in place of all of it" **(types)**). Everything else: `AbovePrompt`. Whether the plugin can tell that its pane is docked when the `Spinner` hook runs is settled by the pane's own `placement` prop **(types)**, kept in an atom.

### E.34 The prompt input · kept

The input box, its rules, autocomplete lists and history search are drawn by the engine. Cockpit styles them through theme colours and the hint line only. The box spans the full terminal width, under both halves.

```text
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> █                                                                             
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  default · shift+tab to change · ? for shortcuts                               
                                                                                
> /st█                                                                          
  ▸ /steps            pin the steps of one turn in the pane · /steps 7 · /steps…
    /status           show version, model and account                           
                                                                                
> review @hooks/reg█                                                            
  ▸ hooks/register.tsx                                                          
    hooks/register.test.tsx                                                     
                                                                                
  history search · "width" · 3 of 12 · ctrl+r for older · esc to leave          
```

- **Empty / typing / multi-line.** `>` at column 0 (the engine's), text from column 2. Cursor in accent.
- **Slash and @ autocomplete.** Engine lists. Theme: selected row on `chip`; command in text-bright, description in muted. Cockpit's commands carry one-line descriptions that fit 60 cells.
- **History search and vim mode** surface in the hint line, state word first.
- **Site.** `PromptHint` (`isDraft` and `isWorking` **(types)**); theme (`$.theme.set`); probe 13.

### E.35 The status line · changed

Two rows, drawn by the status line script, below the prompt, across the full width. Three groups in fixed columns: **who** (model, effort, mode), **where** (repo, branch, changes), **how much** (context, 5-hour window, week). **It keeps its Nerd Font icons**, one per cell, dim, before each group's first word.

```text
   Opus 5.5 · medium · auto         ghostframe ·  fix/ci-bun-pin · 3 uncommitted              +25 −2 lines                                                                                          
   context ▰▰▱▱▱▱▱▱▱▱ 19%           5 hours ▰▱▱▱▱▱▱▱▱▱ 2% · 4h 40m left                        week ▰▰▰▰▱▱▱▱▱▱ 35% · Mon                                                                             
```
```paint
  d bbbb bbb m mmmmmm m WWWW        d tttttttttt m d tttttttttttttt m m mmmmmmmmmmm             d ggg rr mmmmm                                                                                          
  d mmmmmmm tttttttttt ttt          d m mmmmm tttttttttt tt m mm mmm mmmm                       d mmmm tttttttttt ttt m mmm                                                                             
```
```bg
........................................................................................................................................................................................................
........................................................................................................................................................................................................
```

```text
   Opus 5.5 · medium · auto     ghostframe ·  fix/ci-bun-pin            +25 −2                  
   context ▰▰▱▱▱▱▱▱ 19%         5h ▰▱▱▱▱▱▱▱ 2% · 4h 40m                  week 35%                
```

```text
   Opus 5.5 · auto      ghostframe ·  fix/ci-bun-pin     
   context 19%  ·   5h 2%  ·   week 35%                  
```

A limit reached, a permission waiting:

```text
   Opus 5.5 · medium · needs you    ghostframe ·  fix/ci-bun-pin · 3 uncommitted              +25 −2 lines                                                                                          
   context ▰▰▰▰▰▰▰▰▰▱ 92%           5 hours ▰▰▰▰▰▰▰▰▰▰ 100% · resets 16:00                     week ▰▰▰▰▰▰▰▰▱▱ 81% · Mon                                                                             
```

| Width | Row 1 | Row 2 |
|---|---|---|
| 120 and up | model · effort · mode / repo · branch · uncommitted count / line totals | three 10-cell meters with percent and time left or reset day |
| 80–119 | effort stays unless the mode word is long; uncommitted count drops; `lines` word drops | context and 5h keep 8-cell meters; week is a number |
| 60–79 | effort drops; branch is cut with `…`; line totals drop | three numbers, no meters |
| under 60 | model · mode / repo | `ctx 19% · 5h 2% · wk 35%` |

- **Icons** (the mocks use these stand-ins; the script keeps whichever set it has): model U+F2DB, repo U+F07B, branch U+E0A0, changes U+F040, context U+F0E4, 5 hours U+F017, week U+F073. They are decoration beside a word, never the only carrier of meaning, so a font without them loses nothing but looks.
- **Meters.** `▰` filled, `▱` empty. Text colour below 80%, warn from 80%, err from 95%; the percent is always printed.
- **Mode cell.** The same word and colour as E.18; `needs you` in warn replaces it while a permission prompt or question is open.
- **Why there is no context or limits section in the pane.** These three meters are the whole of that information, they are always visible, and principle 10 forbids a second home.
- **Site.** The status line script (not a render site); probe 15.

- **Opening.** `$.ui.open({ id: "cockpit", columns })` at `session.start`, and again whenever the terminal's width or layout changes. The call says whether the pane was placed; when it was not (`isPlaced: false`), Cockpit is in fallback **(types)**.
- **Seat.** The pane's props say `placement: "dock"` or `"inline"` **(types)**. Dock: this section. Inline: Cockpit closes a pane it opened on its own and keeps one you asked for (E.46).
- **Frame.** The engine draws the dock's frame: the divider, and one row of its own that holds the close mark **(types)**. With one pane open the engine draws no title **(types)**. Cockpit draws only the body, `bodyColumns` cells wide **(types)**. The mocks show the divider as `│` and the close mark as `[×]`; their real look is **probe 17**.
- **Background.** None is requested (`backgroundColor` is left out), so both halves sit on the same base **(types)**.
- **What gives way as the pane narrows.**

| Body columns | Step rows | Agent rows | File rows | Source rows |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 72 and up | kind, subject, result (16), clock | name (22), kind, subject, steps, time | path, tag, `+n`, `−n` | mark, host, title, status |
| 56–71 | result shrinks to 14 | name (18) | path cut in the middle | title cut |
| 40–55 | result only on marked rows; subject cut | name (16), no subject | path cut harder | host and status only |
| 24–39 | kind, subject, mark; no clock | name, state word | file name, `+n` | host |

- **Never drawn in the pane:** prose, your words, anything you must act on, a spinner, a wall or border, a background slab (except code and diff rows in the detail views).
- **Site.** `Pane`, by `ui.render` on `{ component: "Pane", requestId: "cockpit" }` **(types)**.

### E.37 The pane: sections, order and fitting · new

**Purpose.** Decide what is shown when everything does not fit, without anything jumping.

**Sections, in fixed order:** `[ NOW ]` (or `[ LAST ]` when idle), `[ STEPS ]`, `[ AGENTS ]`, `[ TODO ]`, `[ FILES ]`, `[ SOURCES ]`. A section with nothing in it is absent. The order is also the priority: if the engine ever clips the pane, it clips from the bottom.

The order is by how fast a thing changes and how soon you need it. `[ NOW ]` changes every second. Steps change every few seconds. Agents and to-dos change by the minute. Files and sources are reference. Nothing with a clock sits below something without one.

**Every section's label rule carries its own summary** (`5 running · 2 done`, `4 of 7`, `11 changed · +418 −388`, `8 read · 5 cited`). So a section can lose all its rows and still say the one thing that matters. That is the collapse step, and it costs nothing.

**The fitted layout.** The pane has `bodyRows` rows **(types)**. Cockpit draws a tree of exactly that height, so the engine never has to scroll it and `[ NOW ]` is always on screen:

1. The head (`[ NOW ]` or `[ LAST ]`) takes 3 rows. It is never dropped.
2. The lower sections are measured first, each up to its cap: `[ AGENTS ]` 7 rows (then `… n more`); `[ TODO ]` 3 rows around the current item (5 in a pane of 40 rows or more); `[ FILES ]` 4 rows, the last being `… n more files` (6 in a tall pane); `[ SOURCES ]` 3 rows (4 in a tall pane).
3. `[ STEPS ]` takes every row that is left, as a window on the step list (E.39). It is the only elastic section.
4. If `[ STEPS ]` would get fewer than 6 rows (3 in a pane under 24 rows), the lower sections give way in this order until it does: the blank rows between sections; `[ SOURCES ]` to its label; `[ FILES ]` to its label; `[ TODO ]` to its current item; `[ AGENTS ]` to running agents only; `[ AGENTS ]` to its label; `[ TODO ]` to its label; then the labels themselves, in the same order.

The ladder, on the model of E.36, at 56 columns and 22, 16, 11 and 7 body rows:

- **Sections do not move during a turn.** The layout is computed when the turn starts and again only when a lower section must grow (the first agent launches, the first file changes). When that happens `[ STEPS ]` gives up rows from its top, where the oldest steps fold into the `… n earlier` row, so the newest steps stay where they were, and everything below the new section's label shifts once. A lower section never shrinks before the turn ends.
- **Does CONTEXT / LIMITS earn a place?** No. The status line has all three meters at every width, the left has the `! needs you` row when one of them needs you, and a third copy would break principle 10. The one useful number the status line lacks, session cost, is one `/cost` away and would make the instrument look like a bill.
- **Height changes.** A change of height alone does not make the engine ask the pane to draw again **(types)**. If the window gets shorter while idle, the engine shows the top of the old tree and the bottom is clipped, which loses the least important section first. The next event (a step, a tick, a key) fits the tree again. Probe 27 measures how long that takes in practice.
- **Site.** `Pane`; `scroll.bodyRows` **(types)**.

### E.38 `[ NOW ]` and `[ LAST ]` · new

- **Label rule.** `[ NOW ]`, and on the right the turn and the number of the step in progress.
- **Row 1, the sentence.** `▸` and the last thing Claude said about the work, in text, cut with `…`. This is where narration lives now: v6's rail sentences. It changes when Claude says something new, which is every few steps, so the eye has something slower than the step row to rest on. Before Claude has said anything it is the fixed phrase for the moment (`Waiting for the first token.`).
- **Row 2, the step.** Kind word (muted, 8 cells), subject (text-bright), the step's own elapsed time on the time edge. Several tools at once: the one that started first, plus `+2`.
- **Not here:** the turn's elapsed time (it is on the left now-row) and a spinner (the one on the left is the only animated cell on screen).
- **Needs you.** The label and row 1 turn to warn: `! needs you · permission` (or `· question · 1 of 2`, `· plan`). Row 2 is the step that asked, and its clock, now in warn, counts how long it has waited. Nothing else in the pane changes colour.

When no turn is running the head is `[ LAST ]`:

  their reports start turn 14                                           
                                                                        
┄ [ LAST ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ turn 14 · 11:31
  done · no steps                                                   0.4s
                                                                        
┄ [ LAST ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ no turn yet
  nothing has run in this session                                       
```

- **Label rule.** `[ LAST ]`, the turn and the wall clock at which it ended.
- **Row 1.** The state word (same word and colour as the receipt), the story in a few words (`1 failure → fixed · ± 7be09d2`), the turn's duration on the time edge.
- **Row 2, only when something is waiting**, in muted: an unresolved failure and what to do about it; background agents still running; the plan's next move. A turn that ended clean has no row 2.
- **What the idle pane shows below it.** The last turn, frozen exactly as it was when it ended: `[ STEPS ]` with its marks, `[ AGENTS ]` with their outcomes, `[ TODO ]` if any item is open or the list finished this turn, `[ FILES ]`, `[ SOURCES ]`. Nothing rearranges at turn end; only the head changes. It is not a session summary: the session's totals are one `/cost` away, and a summary would replace the one thing you look at the pane for after a long turn, which is what just happened.
- **A turn with no steps** (a one-line answer) leaves `[ STEPS ]` showing the most recent turn that had any, and says so in the label: `[ STEPS · TURN 1 ]` (scenario F.2). The pane does not go blank because you asked a quick question.
- **Site.** `Pane`; state from `turn.start`, `turn.step`, `tool.call`, `turn.complete`, and `Spinner`'s `mode` and `message` **(types)**.

┄ [ STEPS ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 12 steps · 2 failed
  read     ci log · job 4211          4s
✕ test     bun te… 2 of 14 fail      41s
  web      "Intl.Segmenter skin…  1m 20s
  fetch    unicode.org/reports/…  1m 31s
  edit     .github/workflows/ci…  1m 44s
⊘ write    tests/wi… classifier   1m 50s
✓ test     bun test    191 pass   2m 31s
± shell    git commit   7be09d2   2m 36s
↗ shell    git push -… 1 commit   2m 40s
✕ mcp      context7…  timed out   2m 44s
∴ thought                            48s
! shell    git push…  needs you   3m 02s
▸ shell    gh pr creat… running   3m 14s
```

- **Row.** Spine mark or blank; kind word (muted, 8 cells); subject (text); result, right-aligned, ending 2 cells before the time edge; the turn clock at which the step finished, dim, on the time edge. Read down the right edge and it is a timeline; read down the left edge and it is the exceptions.
- **Marks.** `✕` failed. `⊘` blocked by a system. `!` waiting on you. `✓` a check that passed after a failure, or the last check before the answer. `±` committed. `↗` published. `▍` you decided this step (denied it, answered it, cut it). `∴` thought. `▸` in progress. A passing step has no mark: 9 of 10 rows are plain grey text.
- **Result colour.** Muted by default. err and bold on a failed row, ok on a `✓` row, warn and bold on `⊘` and `!`, text on `±` and `↗` (a hash or a PR number you may want to read).
- **A step's two states.** A row appears when the step starts (`▸`, result `running`, subject bright) and changes once when it ends (mark, result, clock). After that it never changes. A step that takes less than 250 ms is drawn once, finished.
- **Label rule.** `[ STEPS ]`, then the count and, in err, the number that failed.

**How 300 steps fit.** The section is a window, not a scroll:

- If the steps fit, they are all shown, from the top, with blank rows under them.
- If they do not, the first row is `… n earlier`, followed by up to three kinds by count, in dim. Then come the **open exceptions**: failures and blocks that nothing has fixed yet, however old, at most 3 (if there are more, `✕ 5 open · s shows all`). Then the newest steps, as many as fit. The list scrolls by one each time a step starts, and the `… n earlier` row counts up.
- An exception leaves the pinned rows the moment a later step fixes it. So during a long turn the top of `[ STEPS ]` is a short list of what is still wrong. Usually it is empty.
- The first row never lies about what is hidden: its count and kinds cover exactly the rows that are not shown.

- **Narrow.** E.36's table. At 40 columns a marked row keeps its result and cuts its subject; an unmarked row keeps its subject and drops its result. The mark, the kind word and the clock survive to 40.
- **Site.** `Pane`; `tool.call` (the call, then its result after `next(e)`), `turn.step` **(types)**.

**Purpose.** A live roster. One row per agent from launch to the end of the turn.

◇ Audit helper modules   shell   node redos-probe.mjs       22 ·  1m 12s

  ◇ Verify release dates fetch   registry.npmjs.org          3 ·     18s
◇ Research CI and suppl… web     "zizmor auditor persona"   11 ·  1m 12s
◇ Audit tests            done   3 findings                  31 ·  3m 58s
✕ Audit publish-readine… failed context limit               48 ·  4m 02s
```

v vvvvv vvvvvv vvvvvvv   mmmmm   tttt ttttttttttttttt       dd d  dd ddd
v vvvvvvvv vvvvvvvvv     mmmmm   ttttttttttttttttttt         d d  dd ddd
  v vvvvvv vvvvvvv vvvvv mmmmm   tttttttttttttttttt          d d     ddd
v vvvvvvvv vv vvv vvvvvv mmm     ttttttt ttttttt tttttttt   dd d  dd ddd

```

```text
┄ [ AGENTS ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄ 1 failed · 5 running
◇ Audit register.… read     14 ·  1m 12s
◇ Audit helper mo… shell    22 ·  1m 12s
◇ Research toolch… fetch     9 ·  1m 12s
  ◇ Verify releas… fetch     3 ·     18s
◇ Research CI and… web      11 ·  1m 12s
◇ Audit tests      done     31 ·  3m 58s
✕ Audit publish-r… failed   48 ·  4m 02s
```

- **Row.** `◇` and the name in agent-lavender (22 cells, cut with `…`); the kind word and subject of the agent's current step; its step count and its elapsed time, dim, on the right.
- **States.** *Launched*: kind `start`, subject `reading its brief`, 0 steps. *Running*: as above, the subject changing with each step (at most once a second). *Done*: the glyph and name go muted, the kind slot says `done`, the subject becomes the head of its report (`4 findings`), the time freezes. *Failed*: `✕` in err, `failed` bold, the reason. A row never moves: agents stay in launch order, so you can find one by position.
- **Nested agents** are inset 2 cells under their parent. Two levels are shown; deeper ones are counted on the parent (`+2 nested`).
- **Seven agents** are seven rows. That is the section's cap; an eighth is `… 1 more`, and `a` shows all (E.44). A nested agent rides with its parent and does not count against the cap.
- **Label rule.** Failed first (in err), then running, then done, then nested: at 40 columns the meta loses fields from the right, and the failure is the one to keep.
- **Opening an agent.** Enter on a focused agent row switches the transcript to that agent's own conversation (`$.ui.view`, allowed only as the answer to your own key **(types)**). The pane stays, and its props say which agent is in view **(types)**: `[ STEPS ]` then lists that agent's steps and its label says `[ STEPS · AGENT Audit tests ]`. Enter again, or Escape at the prompt, returns to the main conversation.
- **After the turn.** The roster stays, frozen, until the next turn starts. Background agents that are still running keep their rows live and `[ LAST ]` says so.
- **Site.** `Pane`; `agent.spawn`, `agent.report`, and the `agentId` on `turn.step` and `tool.call` **(types)**.

### E.41 `[ TODO ]` · new

```text
┄ [ TODO ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 4 of 7
  [x] Move the 7 pure-helper tests next to their modules                
  [x] Move hook tests, one module per commit                            
  [x] Keep shared fixtures in tests/kit                                 
  [x] Update the CI glob and the validate ignore list                   
▸ [>] Split register.tsx into ledger and held store                     
  [ ] Grapheme-aware width helper, test first                           
  [ ] Full gate, PR, merge                                              
                                                                        
┄ [ TODO ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 7 of 7
  all 7 done                                                            
```

```text
┄ [ TODO ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 4 of 7
  [x] Update the CI glob and the valida…
▸ [>] Split register.tsx into ledger an…
  [ ] Grapheme-aware width helper, test…
  [ ] Full gate, PR, merge              
```

- **Row.** `[x]` done (muted), `[>]` in progress (text-bright, with `▸` in the spine), `[ ]` open (text). The label rule says `n of m`.
- **Window.** When the list is longer than the section's rows, it shows the item before the current one, the current one, and what follows. Finished items scroll out at the top; the count in the label accounts for them.
- **Rewritten list.** If Claude replaces the list (items added or removed, not just ticked), the section redraws and the label says `REVISED` until the next turn.
- **All done.** One row, `all 7 done`, until the next turn starts; then the section is absent.
- **Site.** `Pane`; `tool.call` on `TodoWrite`.

### E.42 `[ FILES ]` and the diff view · new

**Purpose.** What changed on disk this turn, and one keystroke to see how.

```text
┄ [ FILES ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 11 changed · +418 −388
  hooks/ledger.ts                                        new  +141      
  hooks/held.ts                                          new   +52      
  hooks/register.tsx                                           +11  −238
  hooks/ledger.test.tsx                                  new   +96      
  hooks/held.test.tsx                                    new   +61      
  … 6 more files · +57 −150                                             
```

- **Row.** Path relative to the project root (cut in the middle, keeping the file name); a muted tag, `new`, `del` or `ren`; `+n` in ok and `−n` in err, right-aligned in two columns. In order of first change, so a row never moves; its numbers update when the file is edited again (at most once a second).
- **Scope.** The files this turn changed. Pinned: the files that turn changed. It is not the working tree: the status line has the uncommitted count.
- **Cap.** 4 rows (6 in a tall pane), the last being `… n more files · +a −b`. `f` shows all.

**How a diff is viewed.** Three ways, by where you are:

1. **In the pane.** Focus the pane, Tab to a file, Enter. The pane's body becomes the diff view:

```text
┄ [ DIFF · hooks/register.tsx ]                       +11 −238 · 4 hunks
  hunk 1 of 4 · lines 40 to 52                                          
   import { cellWidth } from "./grid";                                  
  -import { LRU } from "./lru";                                         
  +import { claim, release } from "./ledger";                           
  +import { held } from "./held";                                       
                                                                        
   export const register: Register = (on) => {                          
  -  const ledger = new Map<string, Claim>();                           
  -  const store = new LRU<string, Held>(256);                          
     on("turn.start", ($, e) => {                                       
  -    ledger.clear();                                                  
  +    release(e.turn - 1);                                             
  hunk 2 of 4 · lines 118 to 131                                        
  -    if (ledger.has(key)) return ledger.get(key);                     
  +    return claim(key, e.turn);                                       
                                                                        
  n next hunk   p previous   c copy patch   b back to FILES             
```

   Diff rows are on `surface`, coloured and signed as in E.8. A row longer than the pane wraps with `↩`. `n` and `p` move between hunks, `c` copies the file's patch to the clipboard (`$.ui.copy` **(types)**), `b` or Escape goes back. The view is as tall as the diff: the engine scrolls it with the arrows and the wheel **(types)**.

2. **On the left, on request.** `/diff hooks/register.tsx` prints the first hunk as a diff frame in the transcript, where it can be selected and where a 40-column pane cannot hurt it (scenario F.42). `/diff <path> all` prints every hunk.
3. **In the unfolded view.** ctrl+o shows each edit's diff under its step (E.4).

- **Site.** `Pane`; `tool.call` on the edit and write tools gives the patch; `ui.press` for Enter **(types)**.

### E.43 `[ SOURCES ]` · new

```text
┄ [ SOURCES ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 8 read · 5 cited
  ¹ docs.github.com  Workflow syntax for GitHub Actions              200
  ² docs.github.com  Reusing workflows                               200
  ³ docs.zizmor.sh  Audit rules · personas                           200
  ⁴ docs.npmjs.com  Trusted publishing for npm packages              200
  ⁵ bun.sh  bun publish                                              200
  · github.blog  Changelog: immutable releases                       200
  · securitylab.github.com  Keeping your Actions secure              200
  · docs.sigstore.dev  Cosign · keyless signing                      404
```

```text
┄ [ SOURCES ] ┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄┄ 8 read · 5 cited
  ¹ docs.github.com                  200
  ² docs.github.com                  200
  ³ docs.zizmor.sh                   200
  ⁴ docs.npmjs.com                   200
  ⁵ bun.sh                           200
  · github.blog                      200
  · securitylab.github.com           200
  · docs.sigstore.dev                404
```

- **Row.** The answer's footnote mark in link-blue, or `·` in dim for a page that is not cited; the host in web-teal; the page title in text; the HTTP status on the right, dim, or err and bold when it is not 2xx.
- **Order.** Cited pages first, in mark order, once the answer exists. While the turn runs, pages appear in the order they were fetched, all with `·`. The reorder happens once, when the turn ends and `[ NOW ]` becomes `[ LAST ]`, which is the one moment the pane is allowed to rearrange.
- **Label rule.** `n read · m cited`.
- **No URLs here.** The full, clickable URL of a cited page is in the answer's `[ SOURCES ]` frame on the left (E.9). In the pane, `u` shows every page with its URL on a second row; Enter on one opens it in the browser, `c` copies it (scenario F.41).
- **Site.** `Pane`; `tool.call` on WebFetch and WebSearch; the marks from the final `AssistantMessage`.

### E.44 Focus, keyboard and scrolling · new

**Purpose.** Reach everything the pane holds without the mouse, and never trap a key.

- **Taking and returning the keyboard.** ctrl+x tab or a click gives the pane the keyboard; Escape returns it to the prompt; ctrl+x x closes the pane **(types)**. These are the engine's keys and Cockpit does not rebind them. While the pane has the keyboard its props say `isFocused` **(types)**: the divider turns rule-strong, the engine's row shows `pane`, and the hint line lists the keys (E.18).
- **Moving.** Tab walks the pane's rows that can be opened; the arrows scroll **(types)**. The engine inverts the row that holds the focus ring; the mocks show it on `chip`.
- **Cockpit's keys** are single lower-case letters, which is the only kind a pane may bind **(types)**, and they act only while the pane has the keyboard:

| Key | Does |
|---|---|
| `s` | `[ STEPS ]`: fitted ↔ full view |
| `a` `t` `f` `u` | show all of `[ AGENTS ]`, `[ TODO ]`, `[ FILES ]`, `[ SOURCES ]`; again to fit |
| `p` `n` | pin the previous / next turn (E.45) |
| `l` | live: release the pin |
| enter | open the focused row: a step's detail, a file's diff, an agent's conversation, a source's page |
| `c` | copy the focused row as text (a step's command, a path, a URL, a patch) |
| `b` | back from a detail view |

- **From the prompt**, without focusing: `/steps`, `/diff`, `/files`, `/copy` (E.24).

**The full view.** `s` replaces the fitted layout with the whole step log of the turn: every sentence Claude said (`│` rows) and every step under it, exactly the rows of the unfolded view (E.4), 300 steps and all. The tree is now taller than the pane, and the engine scrolls it **(types)**:

```text
┄ [ STEPS · TURN 7 · ALL ] ┄┄┄┄ 20 steps · 1 failed · rows 1 to 17 of 27
│ Reading the failing job and the test it ran.                          
  read     ci log · job 4211                          212 lines       4s
  read     tests/width.test.tsx                        96 lines       9s
  read     .github/workflows/ci.yml                    61 lines      12s
  search   "setup-bun" in .github                     2 matches      15s
│ Reproducing with the exact Bun version CI used.                       
  shell    docker run oven/bun:1.4.1                     pulled      22s
✕ test     bun test width                          2 of 14 fail      41s
│ It fails here too: 1.4.1 splits the skin-tone modifier.               
  read     hooks/grid.ts                              140 lines      50s
  shell    bun --version                                  1.4.1   1m 02s
│ Checking whether that is ICU or our width helper.                     
  web      "Intl.Segmenter skin tone ICU"             8 results   1m 20s
  fetch    unicode.org/reports/tr29                 200 · 88 kB   1m 31s
  fetch    bun.sh/blog/bun-v1.4.2                   200 · 41 kB   1m 38s
│ Pinning CI to the version in mise.toml.                               
  edit     .github/workflows/ci.yml                       +6 −2   1m 44s
                                                                        
  s fit   c copy row   enter open   p n turn   l live   esc prompt      
```

- The engine follows the end of a tree that is growing, and holds its place once you have scrolled up (**probe 21**). The label says which rows are showing.

- Escape, or `s` again, returns to the fitted layout.

**A step's detail.** Enter on a step:

```text
┄ [ STEP 6 · TURN 7 ]                              2 of 14 fail ·    41s
  test     bun test tests/width.test.tsx --bail                         
  ran      19.2s · exit 1 · 96 lines of output                          
                                                                        
  tests/width.test.tsx:                                                 
  (fail) width > counts a skin-tone modifier with its base [0.38ms]     
    expect(width("👍🏽")).toBe(2) · received 4                            
      at tests/width.test.tsx:41:27                                     
  (fail) width > counts a ZWJ family as one cluster [0.41ms]            
   12 pass                                                              
   2 fail                                                               
  … 89 more lines · ctrl+o shows all                                    
                                                                        
  c copy output   r copy command   b back to STEPS                      
```

- The full command, how long the step itself took, its exit, and the last rows of its output on `surface` (the first rows on success). `c` copies the output, `r` the command.
- **Site.** `Pane` (`isFocused`, `scroll`); `Button` with `hotkey` and `autoFocus`; `ui.press`, `ui.focus`, `ui.scroll`; `$.ui.scroll`, `$.ui.focus`, `$.ui.copy` **(types)**.

### E.45 History: pinning a past turn · new

**Purpose.** The pane shows the current turn, but people scroll back. Pinning puts an earlier turn's steps in the pane without losing sight of the live one.
